[select__712]
sql:
SELECT o.id, d.max_v,
       (SELECT SUM(i.v)
          FROM t2 AS i
         WHERE i.grp = o.grp
         GROUP BY i.grp
        HAVING SUM(i.v) > 0
         ORDER BY SUM(i.v) DESC
         LIMIT 1) AS s
  FROM t1 AS o
  LEFT JOIN (SELECT grp, MAX(v) AS max_v FROM t2 GROUP BY grp) AS d
    ON d.grp = o.grp
 WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t2 AS e WHERE e.id = o.id)
 ORDER BY o.id
 LIMIT 5;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "SELECT": [
    {
      "subject": "Function(2:15~2:18) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/SUM/",
      "action" : "CALL"

      "subject": "Table(3:15~3:17) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/t2/",
      "action" : "READ"

      "subject": "Table(9:7~9:9) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/t1/",
      "action" : "READ"

[select_query_lock__713]
sql:
SELECT o.id FROM t1 AS o
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM DUAL
    WHERE o.id IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY 1
    HAVING COUNT(*) > 0
    ORDER BY 1
    LIMIT 1
    FOR UPDATE
)
ORDER BY o.id
LIMIT 5;

/test/1/catalog1/schema1
